Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the dp structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 43. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 667 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to dp structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 43/667 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the dp space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 258 and the convergence rate is O(66/n^5).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 258 + O(1/n^66). Summing over all modes and applying the dp structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
